028504:     function isAuthoritativeLivePoliceTrainingEntry(
028505:         registryEntry
028506:     ) {
028507:         if (!registryEntry) return false;
028508: 
028509:         const source =
028510:             String(registryEntry.source || '');
028511:         const updatedAt =
028512:             Number(registryEntry.updatedAt || 0);
028513:         const exactRegisterEntry = !!(
028514:             source.startsWith(
028515:                 MF_EXACT_REGISTER_TRAINING_SOURCE_PREFIX
028516:             ) &&
028517:             updatedAt > 0 &&
028518:             Date.now() - updatedAt <=
028519:                 MF_EXACT_REGISTER_TRAINING_MAX_AGE_MS
028520:         );
028521: 
028522:         return !!(
028523:             (
028524:                 isStrictLiveVerifiedTrainingEntry(
028525:                     registryEntry
028526:                 ) ||
028527:                 exactRegisterEntry
028528:             ) &&
028529:             registryEntry.assignmentScanComplete === true &&
028530:             registryEntry.trainingProfilesComplete === true &&
028531:             Array.isArray(
028532:                 registryEntry.assignedTrainingProfiles
028533:             )
028534:         );
028535:     }

028538:     function getProtectedOrdinaryIrvTrainingCount(
028539:         registryEntry
028540:     ) {
028541:         if (!registryEntry) return 0;
028542: 
028543:         const trainingCounts =
028544:             registryEntry.trainingCounts &&
028545:             typeof registryEntry.trainingCounts === 'object'
028546:                 ? registryEntry.trainingCounts
028547:                 : {};
028548: 
028549:         return MF_PROTECTED_ORDINARY_IRV_TRAINING_CODES
028550:             .reduce((total, code) => {
028551:                 return total + Math.max(
028552:                     0,
028553:                     parseInt(trainingCounts[code], 10) || 0
028554:                 );
028555:             }, 0);
028556:     }
028557: 
028558:     function isKnownProtectedTrainedPoliceIrvEntry(
028559:         registryEntry
028560:     ) {
028561:         if (!registryEntry) return false;
028562: 
028563:         const vehicleTypeId =
028564:             String(registryEntry.vehicleTypeId || '');
028565: 
028566:         // Exact MissionChief vehicle IDs are authoritative here. Older shared
028567:         // registry records may pre-date vehicle-type capture, so a missing type
028568:         // must not weaken a positive specialist-training signal.
028569:         return !!(
028570:             (!vehicleTypeId || vehicleTypeId === '8') &&
028571:             getProtectedOrdinaryIrvTrainingCount(
028572:                 registryEntry
028573:             ) > 0
028574:         );
028575:     }

028591:         );

007522:     function sortPersonnelCandidatesForRule(candidates, rule) {
007523:         return candidates.slice().sort((left, right) => {
007524:             const leftReserved = rule.preferWithout.some(code => left.trainingCodes.includes(code)) ? 1 : 0;
007525:             const rightReserved = rule.preferWithout.some(code => right.trainingCodes.includes(code)) ? 1 : 0;
007526:             if (leftReserved !== rightReserved) return leftReserved - rightReserved;
007527: 
007528:             const extraDifference = left.trainingCodes.length - right.trainingCodes.length;
007529:             if (extraDifference) return extraDifference;
007530:             return String(left.name || '').localeCompare(String(right.name || ''), 'en-GB', { numeric: true });
007531:         });
007532:     }

022695:     function renderVehicleLoadListNow() {
022696:         const listContent = document.getElementById('vehicle-load-list-content');
022697:         const patientContent = document.getElementById('vehicle-patient-content');
022698:         const summary = document.getElementById('vehicle-load-summary');
022699:         const progress = document.getElementById('vehicle-load-progress');
022700: 
022701:         if (!listContent || !patientContent || !summary || !progress) return;
022702: 
022703:         refreshVehicleRequirementCounters();
022704: 
022705:         const totalRequired = vehicleLoadState.rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + Number(row.required || 0), 0);
022706:         const foundRequired = vehicleLoadState.rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + Number(row.selected || 0), 0);
022707: 
022708:         const percent = totalRequired > 0 ? Math.round((foundRequired / totalRequired) * 100) : vehicleLoadState.ready ? 100 : 0;
022709: 
022710:         summary.innerHTML = `
022711:             <div>Vehicles: <strong>${foundRequired}</strong> / <strong>${totalRequired}</strong></div>
022712:             <div>Status: <strong class="${vehicleLoadState.ready ? 'mf2026-good' : 'mf2026-warn'}">${vehicleLoadState.ready ? 'Ready' : 'Not Ready'}</strong></div>
022713:         `;
022714: 
022715:         progress.style.width = `${percent}%`;
022716: 
022717:         if (vehicleLoadState.rows.length === 0) {
022718:             listContent.innerHTML = vehicleLoadState.ready
022719:                 ? `<span class="mf2026-good">✓ Ambulance-only mission ready</span>`
022720:                 : `Click Unit Finder to load vehicles.`;
022721:         } else {
022722:             listContent.innerHTML = vehicleLoadState.rows.map(row => {
022723:                 let icon = '○';
022724:                 let cls = 'mf2026-warn';
022725: 
022726:                 const selected = Math.min(Number(row.selected || 0), Number(row.required || 0));
022727:                 const required = Number(row.required || 0);
022728: 
022729:                 if (selected >= required && required > 0) {
022730:                     icon = '✓';
022731:                     cls = 'mf2026-good';
022732:                 } else if (row.status === 'missing') {
022733:                     icon = '✕';
022734:                     cls = 'mf2026-bad';
022735:                 } else if (selected > 0 || row.status === 'retrying') {
022736:                     icon = '↻';
022737:                     cls = 'mf2026-warn';
022738:                 }
022739: 
022740:                 return `

027252:     function getSelectedTrainedPersonnelCountForCode(

027281:                 return total + Math.max(
027282:                     0,
027283:                     parseInt(aggregate?.count, 10) || 0
027284:                 );
027285:             }, 0);

029774:                     rowText

029777:                 rowCodes.add('railway_police');

029834:                 !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
029835:                     trainingCounts,
029836:                     code
029837:                 )
029838:             ) {
029839:                 trainingCounts[code] = 0;
029840:             }
029841:         });

029843:         return {

029851:             assignmentScanComplete:
029852:                 Boolean(personnelTable),
029853:             personnelRowsSeen:
029854:                 personnelRows.length,
029855:             detectedVehicleTypeId,
029856:             document: doc
029857:         };
029858:     }
